Soft gradient may be more direct. Let's design soft optimizer in detail and test mentally.

Pseudo soft_optimize(a0, stop, seed):
```
def soft_optimize(a, stop, seed, beta_start=5, beta_end=500):
 a=normalize(a)
 best=copy, best_sc=score
 rng=...
 for phase in range(phases):
   beta = beta_start*(beta_end/beta_start)**(phase/phases)
   # for each phase, do some iterations
   for it:
     b=_conv_fft(a); M=b.max(); S=1
     # weights: active with exponential. To avoid underflow:
     z = b / max(M,1e-300)
     # Use w = (z - (1-thresh)) positive? Better combine:
     # exponential: logw = beta*(z - 1), z<=1. max 0.
     logw = beta*(z - 1.0)
     # if beta large, many -inf; exp fine.
     w = np.exp(logw - np.max(logw)); w /= w.sum()
     # gradient = 2 * inverse part conv(a, w[::-1])
     grad = _grad_from_weight(a,w)
     # direction: natural update q = a * exp(-step*grad). But grad can be large; step size?
     # Normalize grad by M? grad units? b~M~0.002; grad ~? derivative M wrt a O? If a ~1/n, grad ~? Active average derivative ~M/n ~5e-6? Actually grad_i=2Σactive λ a_{k-i} ~ O(active_count * M? no)
```
Let's estimate: a_i ~1/n≈0.0026 if flat. Active ~O(n) weights sum1, average λ~1/n; grad_i~2/n * sum a ~2/n? ≈0.005. If step lr=1, exp(-0.005) small change. Need lr ~10-100. We can scale grad by M? Since b_k=M coeff; derivative of log M? Gradient of logsumexp scaled by 1/M: grad_log = grad / M ~O(1). Use natural `q = a * exp(-lr * grad / M)`. Then lr ~0.1-1.
```
grad_scaled = grad / max(M,eps)
for step in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2]: q=(a*np.exp(-step*grad_scaled)); normalize; line search t in [0.1,0.3,0.5,1]
```
But if grad_scaled has both high and low, q may underflow. Step small. Also need ensure q sum>0. It will.
   - Alternative direction d = a - (grad_scaled - mean(grad_scaled))*a? q = a*exp(-step*(grad_scaled-mean)) similar. Subtract mean to avoid overall scale? Normalization cancels overall factor, but subtract mean changes relative less? Both okay.
   - We can also use subgradient with threshold: choose top `N_act = max(1, int(0.1*(2n-1)))` weights uniform. Then grad smooth less; maybe robust. Use several active counts: 1, 5, 20, 100. If active few, direction may oscillate. Exponential with beta increasing may combine.
   - For each direction, line search exact over t mixing a and d. The direction d from q maybe close; if no improvement, try smaller step or random perturb.
```
However soft optimization with beta large approximates exact max gradient. It is essentially projected subgradient/natural gradient. It might converge slowly but can polish.

def coordinate_descent(a, stop, seed, max_passes=None):
    a=normalize(a)
    best=copy, best_sc=eval_score(a)

       improved=False
       random or sequential order

           x0=a[i]

           p=a[js]

def _score_coord(x, base_num...):

    if quad: vals[qi] = base_q + x*x - x0*x0
    m = max(vals.max(), unmax)
    S = xb + x
    if S <=0: return inf
    return 2n*m/(S*S)

def _coord_minimize(i, a, b, S, n, max_iter=60):
    x0 = a[i]; xb = S - x0
    if xb <= 1e-300: # only this coord nonzero? set x = x0? but norm sum maybe.
         # Score as x? If only coord, b=[x²] score=2? Actually n length? If other zeros, b[k]=x² at k=2i? length gaps max x², S=x, score=2n. independent x. return x0
    ks = i + np.arange(n)
    base = b[ks]  # length n, view? b maybe np array; base = b[ks].copy? We need not copy? For eval, base +...
    p = a[ks - i] = a  # a array itself; but p for coordinate i at index i? okay.
    quad_j = i
    qi = i  # in p index, ks index i
    # unmax = max(b[~mask]) but mask = ks? need boolean.
    mask = np.zeros(2*n-1, bool); mask[ks]=True
    unmax = b[~mask].max() if len else 0
    # linear coeff for nonquad: 2*p, for quad set 0 to avoid.
    lin = 2.0 * p.copy()
    lin[qi] = 0.0
    base_val = base.copy() # base
    base_quad = base[qi]
    x02 = x0*x0
    const = 2.0*n
    def f(x):
       if x < 0: x=0.0
       dx = x - x0
       vals = base_val + lin*dx   # vector length n
       # vals[qi] currently base[qi] (since lin[qi]=0)
       # set quad
       # To avoid allocate each eval? vals is new; can modify
       vals[qi] = base_quad + x*x - x02
       m = vals.max()
       if unmax > m: m = unmax
       Snew = xb + x
       if Snew <= 1e-300: return inf
       return const * m / (Snew*Snew)
    # coarse candidates
    cands = [0.0, x0]
    # Add multiples of x0
    if x0>0: for m in ... cands += x0*m
    # derivative extrema of individual coefficients: candidate x where f_k ratio extremum?
    # For nonquad, with old base v, lin coefficient l=2p, C=v - l*x0. Ratio extremum: x= S_b - C/p? Since 2p factor: derivative of (C+2p x)/(Sb+x)^2 zero at x = Sb - C/p? C=p? earlier p (coefficient of x in b) =2p_a. derivative: b=C+2p_a x; with p_a variable. Let coef=2p_a=lin. derivative numerator: coef(Sb+x)-2(C+coef x)=coefSb + coef x -2C -2coef x = coefSb -2C - coef x => x=Sb -2C/coef = Sb - C/p_a. Since coef=2p_a, 2C/coef=C/p_a.
       For lin coef>0: C=base_val[j]-lin[j]*x0. x_ext = xb - C/(p_a) maybe. But we can use vector `p_a=p`; `C=base - lin*x0` and `x_ext = xb - C/p_a`. For p_a>0. For quad: C=base_quad - x0²; x_ext = C/xb.
    # We can add finite positive x_ext values, but many. Use quantiles?
```

LP optimizer:
    We can improve over previous. Use `linprog` with scaled B.
```
def _lp_direction(a, M):
   n=len(a)
   if M <=0: return None
   # build A first col, row
   zeros = np.zeros(2*n-1 - n) if 2n-1>n else []? length n-1 zeros.
   col = np.concatenate([a, np.zeros(n-1)])  # length 2n-1
   row = np.concatenate([[a[0]], np.zeros(n-1)]) # length n
   A = np.ascontiguousarray(toeplitz(col,row).astype(np.float64)) / M
   c = -np.ones(n)
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=(0,None)*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'time_limit':?})
   if res.status==0:
       g=res.x; s=g.sum(); if s>eps: return g/s
```

def _coordinate_descent(a0, stop, seed, max_passes=None, tol=1e-12, val_every_pass=True):
    a=normalize(a0)
    b=_conv(a)
    S=1.0
    score=2*n*b.max()
    best=... internal
    rng=...
    # helper update
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       if time>stop-1: break
       order=rng.permutation(n)  # or alternating
       pass_improve=0
       for i in order:
           # update
           newx, new_sc = _coord_minimize_i(i,a,b,S,n)
           if new_sc < score - tol:
               # apply
               oldx=a[i]
               a[i]=newx
               # update b
               ks=i+np.arange(n)
               p=a[:]? p should be old a? If a[i] changed, for linear coefficients p for j != i are old a_j, same. Use old p copy before modifying? p for j includes old a_i at j=i but lin[qi]=0, so we can use a array with new x; p at qi not used. For j≠i, a_j unchanged. Good.
               dx=newx-oldx
               b[ks] += 2.0*a[js??] # careful p should old a_j, but a[j] unchanged; a[i] changed; p[i] = a[i] new but lin[qi] =0 so not used if we set p = a.copy before or mask.

Time management in coordinate_search:
```
def _coordinate_search(a0, stop, seed, max_passes_limit=100000, restart_after_no_improve=30):
   a = normalize(a0); b=conv(a); score=2n*b.max()
   best=copy
   rng=...
   n_passes=0
   no_improve_passes=0
   # Determine pass time strategy: Each pass all n coords. We can decide if enough time for full pass: if time_stop - now > pass_est? Pass_est = max(0.5, (len(a)/384)*1.0) seconds.
   while time < stop - 1 and n_passes < limit:
       if time > stop - pass_est*0.5: break # maybe start pass only if enough time to finish? But coordinate checks per coord; can stop mid-pass and return best; okay. We'll not enforce full pass.
       improved_pass=False
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       for i in order:
           if time > stop - 1: break
           old_score=score
           lx, new_score = _coord_minimize_i(i,a,b,n, max_eval?)
           if new_score < score - 1e-13:
               _apply_coord(i, lx, a,b)
               if score < best_score: update
               improved_pass=True
       n_passes +=1
       # normalize/recompute occasional
       if n_passes % 25 ==0: recompute conv? maybe
       if improved_pass: no_improve=0 else no_improve+=1
       if no_improve >= 15:
           # restart: perturb best, reset current to perturbed.
           scale = 0.05*(1+ n_restarts*0.2)
           start from best with noise
           no_improve=0
```
But if we restart from best with noise, score may temporarily increase; coordinate will reduce. Track best throughout. Need recompute b after perturbation.
   Perturb: choose either small or large; avoid zero. Use alpha (smoothing) maybe: `p = best * exp(normal(0, sigma))`; normalize. Since values ~1/n, absolute sigma = scale * max(np.mean(best), 1/n). If using additive, need ensure nonnegative. Multiplicative lognormal always positive. Use `base = best.copy(); lognoise = np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma)); p=base*lognoise; normalize`. Also maybe random flip/reverse.

def _score(a):
    n=len(a); S=float(a.sum()); if S<0.01: inf; b=_conv(a); return _score_from_b(b,S,n)

`_coord_minimize_i`:
```
def _coord_minimize_i(i, a, b, n, allk=None, maxcands=None):
    x0 = float(a[i]); xb = 1.0 - x0  # sum normalized
    ks = i + np.arange(n)
    base = b[ks].copy()
    un = np.concatenate((allk[:i], allk[i+n:])) if 2*n-1 > i+n else allk[:i]? For i=n-1, allk[i+n:] empty. allk = np.arange(2*n-1)
    if un.size: unmax = float(b[un].max())
    else: unmax=0.0
    p = a  # old normalized
    lin = 2.0 * p.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    lin[i] = 0.0
    base_quad = base[i]
    x02 = x0*x0
    const = 2.0*n
    def f(x):
        if x < 0: x=0.0
        dx = x - x0
        vals = base + lin * dx
        vals[i] = base_quad + x*x - x02
        m = float(vals.max())
        if unmax > m: m = unmax
        Snew = xb + x
        if Snew <= 1e-300: return math.inf
        return const * m / (Snew*Snew)
    old_sc = f(x0) # should equal 2n*b.max?
    xmax = 100.0
    # candidates
    cands = [0.0, x0, xmax]
    if x0 > 0:
       for mfac in (0.125,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.5,2.0,4.0,8.0): cands.append(x0*mfac)
    # xb positive
    if xb > 0:
       for mfac in (0.01,0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,10.0): cands.append(xb*mfac)
    # log grid up to xmax
    low = max(1e-8, min(1e-2, x0 if x0>0 else 1e-4))
    # But if x0 large, okay.
    if xmax > low:
       # 30 log points
       cands.extend(np.logspace(math.log10(low), math.log10(xmax), 30).tolist())
    # derivative ext
    # linear nonquad: p_all = a; valid = p_all>eps and idx!=i
    p_all = a
    valid = p_all > 1e-15
    valid[i] = False
    if np.any(valid):
       C = base - lin * x0  # base - 2p_old x0 (for quad idx lin=0, C=base for idx i, but valid excludes i)
       # p_eff = p_all (since lin=2p)
       with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
           xext = xb - C / p_all
       xext = xext[valid]
       xext = xext[np.isfinite(xext) & (xext > 0) & (xext < xmax)]
       if xext.size:
           xext = np.sort(xext)
           # add 1st,5,10,25,50,75,90,95,99 percent and min max maybe
           for q in (1,5,10,25,50,75,90,95,99):
               cands.append(float(np.percentile(xext, q)))
           cands.extend([float(xext[0]), float(xext[-1])])
    # quad ext
    Cq = base_quad - x02
    if Cq > 0 and xb > 0:
       xq = Cq / xb
       if 0 < xq < xmax: cands.append(xq)
    # Evaluate
    best_x=x0; best_sc=old_sc
    for x in cands:
       x=float(x)
       if not math.isfinite(x) or x<0 or x>xmax: continue
       sc=f(x)
       if sc + 0 < best_sc:
          best_x=x; best_sc=sc
    # refine: local bounded around best if >0
    # We'll do at most 2 minimize_scalar to limit.
    def try_scalar(lo, hi):
       nonlocal best_x,best_sc
       if hi - lo < 1e-12: return
       if lo<0: lo=0.0
       if hi>xmax: hi=xmax
       if hi<=lo: return
       try:
          res = minimize_scalar(f, bounds=(lo,hi), method='bounded', options={'maxiter':25})
          if res.success or res.fun is not None:
             val = res.fun if hasattr(res,'fun') else f(res.x)
             if val < best_sc:
                best_sc=val; best_x=float(res.x)
       except Exception:
          pass
    # local around best
    if best_x > 1e-8:
        width = max(0.25*best_x, 1e-3)
        try_scalar(max(0, best_x-width), min(xmax,best_x+width))
        # also [best_x/2, 2best]
    else:
        try_scalar(0, min(xmax, max(10*best_x, 1e-2, 0.01)))
    # one global if time? Maybe yes but bounded from 0 to xmax can be expensive; but it's 25 eval. Do it.
    try_scalar(0.0, xmax)
    return best_x, best_sc
```
Potential issue: `C = base - lin*x0` for all entries includes quad idx i with lin=0, C=base; valid excludes. If p_all has values very small, xext huge; filter <xmax.
`xmax=100` maybe too small if current coordinate x0=0 and optimum y=0.5 corresponds x=0/?? y=0.5 with x0=0: x = y/(1-y)=1. So x up to 1. If x0=0.9, y=0.99 => x=0.99*(0.1)/0.01=9.9. 100 enough. Good.
But `minimize_scalar` global [0,100] with maxiter 25 might be expensive but okay. It may converge to global if unimodal; if not, okay.

Applying coordinate update:
```
def _apply_coord(i,x,a,b,n):
   old = a[i]
   if old == x: return
   ks = i + np.arange(n)
   dx = x - old
   # We need lin = 2*old a, with lin[i]=0. We can avoid recompute by using a before modification.
   lin = 2.0 * a
   lin[i]=0.0
   b[ks] += lin * dx
   b[2*i] += x*x - old*old
   Snew = 1.0 - old + x
   a[i] = x
   if Snew > 0:
       inv=1/Snew
       a *= inv
       b *= inv*inv
   else: # shouldn't
       a=np.zeros_like(a); a[i]=1.0; b=np.zeros(2*n-1); b[2*i]=1.0
   # clip tiny negative
   np.maximum(b,0,out=b)
```
But note after `a *= inv`, the `lin` used for b update was based on old normalized a. Good. However when we do `b[ks] += lin*dx`, `lin` is a view? `lin = 2.0 * a` creates new array. For i, set 0. Fine. For other indices, old a. Then scaling. Good.
This duplicates code for ks; could pass ks to avoid recompute, but okay.
Potential double update at k=2i: `ks` includes index 2i, and lin[ idx? Wait `ks` is k indices. `lin` is p_j for each j corresponding to j=k-i. The index in ks array corresponding to j=i is i (since ks[i]=2i). We set `lin` array (indexed by j) i to 0. Then `b[ks] += lin*dx` adds for k=2i 0. Then we separately add quad diff. But `b[ks]` indexing: `ks` is array length n; `lin` length n aligned by j. `lin[i]=0`, so at position i in `lin`, k=2i no linear. Good. But after `a *= inv`, for unchanged entries scaling applies. Good.
Score after apply = 2*n*b.max().

Important: If we normalize b after every coordinate, `base` in next coordinate is normalized. Candidate f uses old b normalized. Good.

Coordinate search recompute:

def _recompute(a):
   b=_conv(a); S=a.sum(); if S>0: scale=1/S; a*=scale; b*=scale*scale; return a,b
```
But in coordinate_search, we maintain a sum1. Rewind.
After recompute, score=2n*b.max(). Update best if lower.

`_coordinate_search` implementation:
```
def _coordinate_search(a0, stop, seed, precomp=None, max_passes=1000000, time_per_restart=None):
   a = _normalize(np.asarray(a0,float).copy())
   b = _conv(a)
   # maybe normalize b? a sum1 so b sum1? conv sum=1, tiny okay
   score = 2.0 * len(a) * float(b.max())
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
   n=len(a)
   allk=np.arange(2*n-1)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   pass=0; noimp=0; restarts=0
   noise_scales=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.1,0.05,0.3]
   while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
       # estimate if time for pass: if too little, break? We can break to avoid partial? partial okay but returning best anyway. We'll break if remaining <0.2? For safety.
       if time.monotonic() > stop - 1.0: break
       improved_pass=False
       order = rng.permutation(n) if restarts%2==0 else np.arange(n)
       for i in order:
           if time.monotonic() > stop - 1.0: break
           lx, new_sc = _coord_minimize_i(i, a, b, n, allk)
           if new_sc < score - 1e-13:
               _apply_coord(i, lx, a, b, n)
               score = new_sc  # or 2n b.max for safety? Use new_sc from f; but after normalize/recompute it should match. To be safe: score = 2*n*float(b.max()) after apply? b updated exact, so use 2n max.
               if score < best_score:
                   best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
               improved_pass=True
       pass +=1
       # periodic recompute
       if pass % 20 ==0:
           b = _conv(a)
           # a sum maybe 1; normalize
           s=a.sum(); if s>0: a*=1/s; b*=1/s/s
           score=2*n*float(b.max())
           if score < best_score: best...
       if improved_pass: noimp=0
       else:
           noimp +=1
           if noimp >= 8:
               noimp=0
               restarts +=1
               # restart from best, maybe with noise or power
               scale_idx = min(restarts-1 if restarts>0 else 0, len(noise_scales)-1)
               sig = noise_scales[(restarts-1)%len(noise_scales)] if restarts>1 else 0.05
               # choose method
               if restarts % 3 == 1:
                   p = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0, sig, n))
               elif restarts % 3 == 2:
                   p = np.clip(best_a + sig*np.mean(best_a)*rng.normal(0,1,n),0,None)
               else:
                   p = best_a ** (1 + sig)  # power maybe
               s=sum(p); if s<1e-12: p=best_a.copy()
               p/=s; b=_conv(p); a=p; score=2*n*float(b.max())
               # also maybe reverse if no improvement?
               # If restart too far and score > best+1, maybe fallback to best small noise
               if score > best_score + 1.0:
                   p = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0, min(sig,0.05), n)); normalize
       # If time left less than estimated pass, break? continue until stop; coordinate checks each coord.
   # final recompute exact b
   b=_conv(a); s=a.sum(); if s>0 normalize
   score=2*n*float(b.max())
   if score < best_score: best...
   # Ensure best_a sum1
   s=best_a.sum(); if s>0: best_a/=s
   return best_a, best_score
```

In `_lp_direction`, build A scaled. If M is very small relative f? f normalized sum1; b max ~M. `f_scaled = a / M`, but if M=0? return None. A entries O(1). brhs=rhs/M. b may have tiny negatives; clip. Code:
```
rhs = np.maximum(b,0.0)
rhs = (1.0-tight)*M + tight*rhs
eps = 1e-12 * M + 1e-15
rhs = np.maximum(rhs, eps)
A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
fs = a / M
for c in range(n): A[c:c+n,c] = fs
res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs/M, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, ...)
```

_fast = {}
def _conv(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    fs = _fast.get(n)
    if fs is None:
       fs = next_fast_len(2*n - 1)
       _fast[n] = fs
    F = rfft(a, fs)
    c = irfft(F * F, fs)[: 2*n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```

```
def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15 or a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(384 if a.size==0 else a.size, dtype=np.float64) / (384 if a.size==0 else a.size)
    return a / s
```

`try_scalar`:
```
def try_scalar(lo,hi):
   nonlocal best_x,best_sc
   lo=float(lo); hi=float(hi)
   if lo < 0: lo=0.0
   if hi > xmax: hi=xmax
   if hi <= lo + 1e-12: return
   # avoid x0=1 invalid at 0
   if lo == 0.0 and xb <= 1e-14: lo = 1e-12
   try:
       res = minimize_scalar(f, bounds=(lo,hi), method='bounded', options={'maxiter': 18 if quick else 25})
       val = float(res.fun) if hasattr(res,'fun') and res.fun is not None else f(float(res.x))
       if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_sc:
           best_sc=val; best_x=float(res.x)
   except Exception: pass
```

if not quick:

Apply:
```
def _apply_coord(i,x,a,b,n):
    old=float(a[i]); x=float(x)
    if abs(x-old) <= 1e-15 and (x==0 or old==0): return? still maybe no.
    ks = i + np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    dx=x-old
    lin=2.0*a
    lin[i]=0.0
    b[ks] += lin*dx
    b[2*i] += x*x - old*old
    Snew = 1.0 - old + x
    a[i]=x
    if Snew > 1e-300:
        inv=1.0/Snew
        a *= inv
        b *= inv*inv
    else:
        a.fill(0.0); a[i]=1.0; b.fill(0.0); b[2*i]=1.0
    # clip
    np.maximum(a,0,out=a); np.maximum(b,0,out=b)
```
If old=1, x=0, Snew=0, falls to a[i]=1 (unchanged). But f(0) inf; not accepted. If due numerical accepted? no.

In coordinate_search, after pass and periodic recompute, if score improves more than tol update best. But if recompute score is slightly higher than tracked since last best, no issue.

But it updates best only when trajectory improves below current best. If a perturbation makes score worse but then coordinate descent crosses to a lower basin without ever below original best? It must pass below original best to update; okay. It can.

Now, Phase allocation code. Let's write carefully:

def propose_candidate(...):
    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=max(10.0,float(budget_s)); deadline=t0+budget-20.0
    parent=_get_parent(); parent=_norm(parent)
    best_a=parent.copy(); best_exact=math.inf
    def update(a): ...
    update(best_a)
    n0=best_a.size

    # inits
    inits=[]
    def add_init(arr):
       arr=_norm(arr); sc=_score_vec(arr); inits.append((sc, arr))
    add_init(parent)
    p=parent.copy()
    if p.sum()>0:
       add_init(p**2)
       add_init(np.sqrt(p))
    add_init(_sqrt_init(n0))
    # lopsided: exp(-i/(n*0.5))
    ii=np.arange(n0, dtype=float)
    add_init(np.exp(-ii/(n0*0.5)))
    add_init(np.exp(-((ii-n0*0.5)/(n0*0.25))**2))
    # sort by score? Keep parent first maybe. We can sort by score, but parent may not have best score? internal. Use sorted.
    inits.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    # But parent might not be top; we want run good inits. Sorting okay. If parent score 1.509, sqrt 1.57, parent top. If power better, run it.
    # Use top 4 unique? If duplicate, still.
```
Sorting initializations by internal score may run best parent first. Good.

Before final, one more thought: The evaluator score uses `max_b = max(b_sequence)`, where b_sequence from `np.convolve` with sequence clipped nonnegative and min 1000. It does not enforce sum normalization; our normalized sum1. Good.

```python
import math
import time

import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize_scalar

_fast_fft_cache = {}


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)

    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    np.maximum(p, 0.0, out=p)
    np.minimum(p, 1000.0, out=p)

    if p.size == 0 or float(p.sum()) < 1e-12:
        n = p.size if p.size else 384
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64) / 384.0
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / a.size
    return a / s


def _conv(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    fs = _fast_fft_cache.get(n)
    if fs is None:
        fs = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        _fast_fft_cache[n] = fs
    F = rfft(a, fs)
    c = irfft(F * F, fs)[: 2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_from_b(b, s, n):
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    m = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = max(0.0, m) if math.isfinite(m) else math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _score_vec(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return math.inf
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    b = _conv(a)
    return _score_from_b(b, s, a.size)


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _sqrt_init(n):
    if n <= 0:
        n = 384
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        u[i] = u[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        u = np.ones(n)
        s = float(n)
    return u / s


def _interp_init(seq, L):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if L <= 0:
        return seq.copy()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 1:
        return np.full(L, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    y = seq
    out = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L), x, y)
    return _norm(out)


def _build_lp_matrix(a, M):
    n = a.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return A
    fs = a / M
    for c in range(n):
        A[c:c + n, c] = fs
    return A


def _lp_direction(a, b, M, tight):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    bpos = np.maximum(np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    rhs = (1.0 - tight) * M + tight * bpos
    eps = 1e-12 * M + 1e-15
    np.maximum(rhs, eps, out=rhs)
    rhs /= M

    A = _build_lp_matrix(a, M)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bnd = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=rhs,
            bounds=bnd,
            method="highs",
            options={
                "presolve": True,
                "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-8,
                "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-8,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return None
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return g / s


def _lp_optimize(a0, stop, seed):
    try:
        a = _norm(a0).copy()
    except Exception:
        return a0, math.inf

    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0, math.inf

    b = _conv(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    score = float(2.0 * n * M)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = 0.35
    tight = 0.55
    stall = 0
    last_good = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 2.0:
        if M <= 1e-300:
            break

        g = _lp_direction(a, b, M, tight)

        if g is None:
            stall += 5
            if stall > 220:
                break
            noise = 0.07 * max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-6)
            a = _norm(a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            b = _conv(a)
            M = float(b.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * M)
            continue

        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g - a))
        if gn < 1e-10:
            stall += 3
            if stall > 220:
                break
            noise = 0.08 * max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-6)
            a = _norm(a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            b = _conv(a)
            M = float(b.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * M)
            continue

        cur = score
        best_sc = cur
        best_ft = None
        best_t = 0.0

        steps = [0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0]
        steps.extend([t, max(0.05, min(1.0, t * 0.5)), max(0.05, min(1.0, t * 1.7))])

        for tt in steps:
            tt = float(max(0.0, min(1.0, tt)))
            cand = (1.0 - tt) * a + tt * g
            cand = _norm(cand)
            sc = _score_vec(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc
                best_ft = cand
                best_t = tt

        if best_ft is not None:
            a = best_ft
            # local 1-D refinement in mix coordinate
            try:
                def mix_score(x):
                    x = float(max(0.0, min(1.0, x)))
                    cand = _norm((1.0 - x) * a + x * g)
                    return _score_vec(cand)

                lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.18)
                hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.18)
                if hi - lo > 1e-9:
                    res = minimize_scalar(mix_score, bounds=(lo, hi), method="bounded",
                                          options={"maxiter": 18})
                    if res.fun is None or not math.isfinite(res.fun):
                        raise RuntimeError("bad fun")
                    if res.fun < best_sc - 1e-15:
                        best_sc = float(res.fun)
                        a = _norm((1.0 - float(res.x)) * a + float(res.x) * g)
            except Exception:
                pass

            b = _conv(a)
            M = float(b.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * M)

            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_good = time.monotonic()

            stall = 0
            t = min(1.0, max(0.08, best_t))
            tight = min(1.0, tight + 0.12)
        else:
            stall += 1
            t = max(0.05, t * 0.55)
            tight = max(0.0, tight - 0.25)
            if stall == 30 or stall % 50 == 0:
                noise = 0.05 * max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-6) + 1e-8
                a = _norm(a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
                b = _conv(a)
                M = float(b.max())
                score = float(2.0 * n * M)
                stall = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_good > 180.0 and stall > 80:
            break

    b = _conv(best_a)
    M = float(b.max())
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
    if best_score < _score_vec(a):
        best_a = _norm(a)
        best_score = _score_vec(best_a)
    return best_a, best_score


def _coord_minimize_i(i, a, b, n, allk=None, quick=False):
    if allk is None:
        allk = np.arange(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)

    x0 = float(a[i])
    xb = 1.0 - x0
    lower = 0.0 if xb > 1e-14 else 1e-12
    xmax = 50.0 if quick else 100.0

    ks = i + np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    base = b[ks].copy()

    un = np.concatenate((allk[:i], allk[i + n:]))
    unmax = float(b[un].max()) if un.size else 0.0

    lin = 2.0 * a
    lin[i] = 0.0

    base_quad = float(base[i])
    x02 = x0 * x0
    const = 2.0 * n

    def f(x):
        x = float(x)
        if not math.isfinite(x) or x < 0.0:
            return math.inf
        dx = x - x0
        vals = base + lin * dx
        vals[i] = base_quad + x * x - x02
        m = float(vals.max())
        if unmax > m:
            m = unmax
        Snew = xb + x
        if Snew <= 1e-300:
            return math.inf
        return const * m / (Snew * Snew)

    old_sc = f(x0)
    if not math.isfinite(old_sc):
        old_sc = math.inf

    best_x = x0 if 0.0 <= x0 <= xmax else 0.0
    best_sc = old_sc

    cands = []
    if xb > 1e-14:
        cands.append(0.0)
    cands.append(x0)
    cands.append(xmax)

    if x0 > 1e-15:
        for mfac in (0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.5, 2.0, 4.0):
            cands.append(x0 * mfac)

    if xb > 1e-15:
        for mfac in (0.01, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
            cands.append(xb * mfac)

    if xmax > 1e-8:
        lo = max(1e-8, min(1e-2, max(x0, 1e-4)))
        try:
            npts = 8 if quick else 25
            cands.extend(np.logspace(math.log10(lo), math.log10(min(xmax, max(10.0 * lo, 1e-6))), npts).tolist())
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Candidate extrema from individual one-coordinate fractions.
    valid = a > 1e-15
    valid[i] = False
    if np.any(valid):
        C = base - lin * x0
        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            xext = xb - C / a
        xext = xext[valid]
        xext = xext[np.isfinite(xext) & (xext > 0.0) & (xext < xmax)]
        if xext.size:
            if not quick:
                for q in (1, 5, 10, 25, 50, 75, 90, 99):
                    try:
                        cands.append(float(np.percentile(xext, q)))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            cands.append(float(xext[0]))
            cands.append(float(xext[-1]))

    Cq = base_quad - x02
    if Cq > 0.0 and xb > 1e-14:
        xq = Cq / xb
        if 0.0 <= xq <= xmax:
            cands.append(float(xq))

    evaluated = []
    for x in cands:
        try:
            x = float(x)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not math.isfinite(x) or x < lower or x > xmax:
            continue
        sc = f(x)
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        evaluated.append((sc, x))
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_x = x

    def try_scalar(lo, hi):
        nonlocal best_x, best_sc
        try:
            lo = float(lo)
            hi = float(hi)
        except Exception:
            return
        if lo < 0.0:
            lo = 0.0
        if hi > xmax:
            hi = xmax
        if hi <= lo + 1e-12:
            return
        if lo == 0.0 and xb <= 1e-14:
            lo = 1e-12
            if hi <= lo:
                return
        try:
            res = minimize_scalar(
                f,
                bounds=(lo, hi),
                method="bounded",
                options={"maxiter": 14 if quick else 22},
            )
            val = float(res.fun) if res.fun is not None else f(float(res.x))
            if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_sc:
                best_sc = val
                best_x = float(res.x)
        except Exception:
            pass

    evaluated.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    if evaluated:
        topk = 1 if quick else 2
        for sc, x in evaluated[:topk]:
            if x > 1e-10:
                width = max(0.22 * x, 1e-4)
                try_scalar(max(lower, x - width), min(xmax, x + width))
            else:
                try_scalar(lower, min(xmax, max(1e-3, 0.05)))

    if not quick:
        try_scalar(lower, xmax)

    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        return x0 if 0.0 <= x0 <= xmax else lower, old_sc
    return max(0.0, float(best_x)), float(best_sc)


def _apply_coord(i, x, a, b, n):
    old = float(a[i])
    x = float(x)
    if abs(x - old) <= 1e-16:
        return

    ks = i + np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    dx = x - old
    lin = 2.0 * a
    lin[i] = 0.0

    b[ks] += lin * dx
    b[2 * i] += x * x - old * old

    Snew = 1.0 - old + x
    a[i] = x

    if Snew > 1e-300:
        inv = 1.0 / Snew
        a *= inv
        b *= inv * inv
    else:
        a.fill(0.0)
        a[i] = 1.0
        b.fill(0.0)
        b[2 * i] = 1.0

    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)


def _coordinate_search(a0, stop, seed, quick=False):
    a = _norm(a0).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0, math.inf

    b = _conv(a)
    score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(b.max())))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    allk = np.arange(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve_passes = 0
    restarts = 0
    pass_count = 0
    tol = max(1e-13, 1e-11 * max(1.0, score))

    noise_scales = [0.03, 0.08, 0.15, 0.30, 0.55, 0.20, 0.45, 0.10, 0.70]

    def restart_from_best():
        nonlocal a, b, score, no_improve_passes, restarts
        restarts += 1
        base = best_a.copy()
        sig = noise_scales[(restarts - 1) % len(noise_scales)]
        method = restarts % 4

        if method == 1:
            p = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
        elif method == 2:
            std = sig * max(float(np.mean(base)), 1.0 / max(n, 1))
            p = np.clip(base + rng.normal(0.0, std, n), 0.0, None)
        elif method == 3:
            expv = 1.0 + (sig if restarts % 2 else -sig)
            np.maximum(base, 1e-300, out=base)
            p = base ** expv
        else:
            rev = base[::-1].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
            p = rev * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, min(sig, 0.25), n))

        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            p = base

        p = p / float(p.sum())
        nb = _conv(p)
        nsc = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(nb.max())))

        if (not math.isfinite(nsc)) or nsc > best_score + 0.7:
            small = 0.02
            p = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, small, n))
            p = p / float(p.sum())
            nb = _conv(p)
            nsc = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(nb.max())))

        a = p.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
        b = nb.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
        score = nsc if math.isfinite(nsc) else best_score
        no_improve_passes = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.5:
        pass_improved = False
        use_random_order = (restarts % 2 == 0)
        order = rng.permutation(n) if use_random_order else np.arange(n)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1.5:
                break
            old_score = score
            nx, nsc = _coord_minimize_i(int(i), a, b, n, allk, quick=quick)

            if math.isfinite(nsc) and nsc < old_score - tol:
                _apply_coord(int(i), nx, a, b, n)
                score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(b.max())))
                if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                pass_improved = True

        pass_count += 1

        if pass_count % 12 == 0 or not pass_improved:
            try:
                b = _conv(a)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-12:
                    a = a / s
                    b = b / (s * s)
                score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(b.max())))
                if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
            except Exception:
                b = _conv(a)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-12:
                    a = a / s
                    b = b / (s * s)
                score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(b.max())))

        if pass_improved:
            no_improve_passes = 0
        else:
            no_improve_passes += 1
            if no_improve_passes >= (4 if quick else 7):
                restart_from_best()

    try:
        b = _conv(best_a)
        s = float(best_a.sum())
        if s > 1e-12:
            best_a = best_a / s
            b = b / (s * s)
        final_score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(b.max())))
    except Exception:
        best_a = _norm(best_a)
        final_score = _score_vec(best_a)

    if math.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
    elif not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = final_score

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget) - 20.0

    parent = _norm(_get_parent())
    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_exact = _exact_score(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_a = _norm(np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64))
        best_exact = _exact_score(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_a = _norm(np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64))
        best_exact = _exact_score(best_a)

    def update_candidate(a):
        nonlocal best_a, best_exact
        if a is None:
            return
        arr = _norm(a)
        if arr.size == 0:
            arr = _norm(np.ones(384))
        sc = _exact_score(arr)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_a = arr.copy()

    n0 = int(best_a.size)

    # Initial candidate pool.
    inits = []

    def add_init(arr):
        try:
            arr = _norm(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
            if arr.size:
                inits.append((float(_score_vec(arr)), arr))
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_init(parent)
    add_init(parent ** 2)
    add_init(np.sqrt(parent))
    add_init(_sqrt_init(n0))

    ii = np.arange(n0, dtype=np.float64)
    add_init(np.exp(-ii / max(1.0, 0.55 * n0)))
    add_init(np.exp(-((ii - 0.5 * n0) / max(1.0, 0.25 * n0)) ** 2))
    add_init(0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * ii / max(1.0, n0 - 1)))
    add_init(0.4 + 0.6 * np.sin(np.pi * ii / max(1.0, n0)))

    inits = [x for x in inits if math.isfinite(x[0])]
    inits.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    # Phase A: coordinate search from several initializations.
    phaseA_stop = t0 + budget * 0.17
    phaseA_dur = max(1.0, phaseA_stop - t0)
    allocs = [0.46, 0.25, 0.19, 0.10]
    for idx, (_, init_a) in enumerate(inits[:4]):
        if time.monotonic() >= phaseA_stop - 2.0:
            break
        dur = phaseA_dur * (allocs[idx] if idx < len(allocs) else 0.06)
        stop = min(phaseA_stop, time.monotonic() + dur)
        if stop - time.monotonic() < 2.0:
            continue
        try:
            cand, _ = _coordinate_search(init_a, stop, int(seed) + 10 + idx, quick=False)
            update_candidate(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase B: LP-guided move on the incumbent.
    phaseB_stop = t0 + budget * 0.38
    if n0 <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < phaseB_stop - 15.0:
        stop = min(phaseB_stop, deadline - 80.0)
        try:
            cand, _ = _lp_optimize(best_a, stop, int(seed) + 1000)
            update_candidate(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase C: focused coordinate polishing.
    phaseC_stop = t0 + budget * 0.60
    if time.monotonic() < phaseC_stop - 10.0:
        phaseC_rem = max(0.0, phaseC_stop - time.monotonic())
        stop1 = min(phaseC_stop, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, 0.58 * phaseC_rem))
        try:
            cand, _ = _coordinate_search(best_a, stop1, int(seed) + 30000, quick=False)
            update_candidate(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() < phaseC_stop - 5.0:
            try:
                cand, _ = _coordinate_search(best_a[::-1], phaseC_stop - 2.0, int(seed) + 31000, quick=False)
                update_candidate(cand)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Phase D: more restarts and a few other lengths.
    phaseD_stop = t0 + budget * 0.82
    if time.monotonic() < phaseD_stop - 15.0:
        phaseD_rem = max(0.0, phaseD_stop - time.monotonic())
        stop1 = min(phaseD_stop, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, 0.45 * phaseD_rem))
        try:
            cand, _ = _coordinate_search(best_a, stop1, int(seed) + 50000, quick=False)
            update_candidate(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() < phaseD_stop - 10.0:
            lengths = [256, 320, 448, 512, 640, 768, 1024]
            lengths = [L for L in lengths if 128 <= L <= 1024 and abs(L - n0) >= 64]
            if len(lengths) < 3:
                lengths.append(512 if n0 != 512 else 640)

            remaining = max(0.0, phaseD_stop - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
            if remaining > 10.0 and lengths:
                per = max(5.0, remaining / max(1, len(lengths)))
                for L in lengths:
                    if time.monotonic() >= phaseD_stop - 5.0:
                        break
                    stop = min(phaseD_stop - 2.0, time.monotonic() + per)
                    if stop - time.monotonic() < 3.0:
                        continue
                    try:
                        initB = _interp_init(best_a, int(L))
                        quickL = (L > 768) or (per < 35.0)
                        cand, _ = _coordinate_search(initB, stop, int(seed) + 70000 + int(L), quick=quickL)
                        update_candidate(cand)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
    # Phase E: final deterministic polish on the current best.
    final_stop = deadline - 12.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_stop - 5.0:
        try:
            cand, _ = _coordinate_search(best_a, final_stop, int(seed) + 99000, quick=False)
            update_candidate(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final validation.
    try:
        final_a = _norm(best_a)
        final_sc = _exact_score(final_a)
    except Exception:
        final_a = None
        final_sc = math.inf

    if final_a is None or final_a.size == 0 or not math.isfinite(final_sc):
        try:
            final_a = _norm(parent)
            final_sc = _exact_score(final_a)
        except Exception:
            final_a = np.ones(384) / 384.0
            final_sc = _exact_score(final_a)

    if final_a is None or final_a.size == 0 or not math.isfinite(final_sc):
        final_a = np.ones(384) / 384.0

    final_a = _norm(final_a)
    final_a = np.clip(np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final_a.size == 0 or final_a.sum() < 0.01:
        final_a = np.ones(384) / 384.0

    out = [float(x) for x in final_a]
    try:
        fin = _exact_score(out)
        if not math.isfinite(fin):
            out = [float(x) for x in (np.ones(384) / 384.0)]
    except Exception:
        out = [float(x) for x in (np.ones(384) / 384.0)]

    return out
```
